Alternative: We can implement the iterative method without LP by using projections. For fixed f and M, maximizing ∑g subject to f*g<=M, g>=0. This LP can be solved by greedy? Since f >=0 and M>0. The constraints are all forward/backward sums with nonnegative kernels. Because f nonnegative, if we assign g mass, it contributes to a contiguous set of constraints. For decreasing f, the constraints windows have monotone weights. The LP optimal g might be obtainable by greedy: assign mass to variable j that has the most "cheap" capacity? Let's analyze.

Primal: variables g_j each contribute to constraints r=j..j+n-1 with weight f_{r-j}=f_{0..n-1} (decreasing from f0 to f_{n-1}). So early variables j contribute more heavily to early constraints (small r) because f_{r-j} larger when r-j small. To maximize total mass under per-constraint capacities M, we should put mass in variables that use slack constraints. This is like scheduling intervals with weighted resource usage. The optimal can be found by linear programming; maybe greedy: sort variables by something. If f is decreasing, variable j's contribution vector is a shifted copy of f. The "earliest" variables use capacity in early constraints; later use later. Since early constraints perhaps more tight (for left-flat, first n tight), we should put mass late. The optimum g might be concentrated at end. For n=2 uniform, f=[0.5,0.5], constraints tight all; max sum= S; no unique. cap = array length 2n-1 all M.
For j from n-1 downto 0:
  max_g = min_{r=j}^{j+n-1} cap[r]/f[r-j] (if f>0)
  g_j = max_g (or maybe damped)
  for r=j..j+n-1: cap[r] -= f[r-j]*g_j
This is O(n^2) =23M operations; in Python loops too slow, but vectorized per j O(n) with numpy inner loop 4806*4806 ~23M element operations in Python-level loops (4806 iterations, each numpy slice) maybe okay: each slice assignment length average 2400 -> 23M, 4806 Python loop iterations; could take ~0.5-1s. Implementing greedy vectorized:
def lp_greedy(f, M, n, f_pre?):
  cap = np.full(2*n-1, M)
  g = np.zeros(n)
  # f nonnegative length n; assume f[0]>0.
  for j in range(n-1,-1,-1):
     # weights = f[0:n] (since r-j = 0..n-1)
     # ratio = cap[j:j+n] / f[:n], but f zeros? Use where f>0.
     # due to floating cap may negative tiny; cap_pos = np.maximum(cap[j:j+n],0)
     ratios = cap[j:j+n] / f
     max_g = ratios.min()
     if max_g < 0: max_g=0
     g[j]=max_g
     cap[j:j+n] -= f*max_g
  return g

This uses f same for all j. But f weights from r-j=0..n-1; yes.

Good. cap slice length n. 4806*4806 ~23M, fine. But 4806 Python iterations; maybe 0.5s. If run 20 times =10s.

Objective function:
```
def obj_grad(z):
    amax = np.max(z)
    a = np.exp(z - amax)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= tiny: return np.inf, None
    a /= s
    C = fft_conv_same(a)
    # scaled logsumexp
    m = C.max()
    # to avoid β* differences under/overflow: c = beta*(C - m)
    c = beta*(C - m)
    # if beta large, c max 0, min maybe -1e?; use sum exp
    # weights for gradient: exp(c) normalized; but sum exp maybe K if c ~0; K up to 2n ~10k, safe.
    w = np.exp(c)
    zw = w.sum()
    M = m + np.log(zw)/beta
    # grad_M = 2*corr(a,w/zw)
    w /= zw
    gM = corr(a,w)
    dot = (a*gM).sum()
    ham = gM - dot  # gradient of M wrt a on simplex
    # grad wrt z: a * ham - lam * (mean z) * ones/n? plus penalty
    gradz = a * ham
    if lam: gradz += lam * np.mean(z) / n
    val = M  # or log? Use M; constants
    return val + lam * np.mean(z)**2? maybe, gradz
```
Using M (not log) since sum=1; score=2nM. Minimizing M. Good.

But if C has values slightly below m by 1e-8, β=100k -> exp(-1) weight; fine. If below by 1e-5 -> exp(-1000) zero; near max only. This approximates active set.

Implementation details for FFT:
```
def next_pow2(x): return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
N = next_pow2(3*n - 2)
def conv_same(a):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    ca = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)
    return ca[:2*n-1]
def cross(a,w):
    ar = a[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    cw = np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, N)
    return cw[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check `fw` length N; w length 2n-1. We can create w padded each time; `np.fft.rfft(w, N)` pads zeros. Need ensure `irfft` output length N. Good.

Because a and w real nonnegative, C and grad real. Use `np.ascontiguousarray` maybe.

Custom subgradient implementation:
```
def subgradient_search(a, deadline, max_iter, tol_ratio=0.02, lr=0.1, rng):
    best=a.copy(); bestM=conv_max(best)
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time>deadline break
       C=conv_same(a); M=C.max()
       if 2*n*M < bestM_score: update best
       w=zeros; active = C >= M*(1-tol_ratio)
       if count==0: active[argmax]=True
       if count > max_active? (e.g., 5000) no
       w[active]=1/count
       g=corr(a,w)
       dot=(a*g).sum(); h=g-dot
       # adaptive lr: maybe step size scaled by norm of h? 
       # Use Adam? Simpler exp(-lr*h). But h scale varies.
       step = lr * h
       # limit
       a_new = a * np.exp(np.clip(-step, -4,4))
       a_new = np.maximum(a_new, tiny); a_new/=a_new.sum()
       Cnew=conv_same(a_new); Mnew=Cnew.max()
       if Mnew < M:
           a=a_new; lr=min(2*lr, 1.0)
       else:
           # if small increase allow?
           if Mnew < M*1.001:
               a=a_new
           else:
               lr*=0.5
               if lr<1e-4:
                   # reheat/noise
                   a=best.copy()*(1+0.02*rand?)
                   lr=0.05
       if it%100==0: exact eval, update best
    return best
```

```
def _left_flat(n):
    a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
    a/=a.sum(); return a
```

Implement coarse polish:
- Let c = exp(z)/sum (length K). a_full = c_block / sum. Block index array `block_id` length n mapping each position to segment.
- Given grad_full gM projected on a_full simplex. Need gradient of M(a_full) wrt z_c. First, M'(a_full)=gM. Scale fixed? a_full = E(c) where E adds repeats; sum a_full = sum block_len_j c_j. To normalize: a_full = E(c)/S. It's a softmax-like map from z to a_full. Derivative: ∂a_i/∂z_p = δ_{block(i),p}/S - a_i * L_p/S, where L_p=block_len_p c_p (since ∂c_p/∂z_p = c_p(1-c_p) but after E/s?). Let's derive simpler: a = E(exp(z))/S. Pseudo full:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
import scipy.optimize as spopt

def _next_pow2(m): ...
def _left_flat(n): ...
def _make_fft_ops(n): ...
def _norm(a, floor=1e-300):
    a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,float),0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return None
    a=a/s
    # cap? sum 1 so cap no
    return a

def _score_eval(a):
    try:
       s=evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
    except Exception:
       s=float('inf')
    return s

def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(1,float(budget_s)-10)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   # parent
   try: par=height_sequence_1
   except NameError: par=None
   par_arr=None
   if par is not None:
       p=np.asarray(par,dtype=float)
       if p.ndim==1 and p.size>0:
          p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
          p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
          if p.sum()>0.01: par_arr=p
   if par_arr is not None and par_arr.size>=50:
       n=par_arr.size
   else:
       n=4806
   # If n too large for memory? Limit maybe 12000. If parent huge, downsample? Use min(parent size, 8192)? But length affects score; if parent huge, use 8192? Problem likely 4806. Set if n>10000: n=10000? But parent length fixed. We can use n = min(n, 10000) to protect. But then length different than parent; okay. Use 8192 maybe. If n<2: n=4806.
   if n < 2: n=4806
   if n > 10000: n=10000  # safety

   N, conv_same, cross = _make_fft_ops(n)

   a_left = _left_flat(n)
   # candidates list
   candidates=[]
   if par_arr is not None and par_arr.size == n:
      par_norm=_norm(par_arr); if par_norm: candidates.append(par_norm)
   # if par size != n, maybe evaluate parent separately with its length? Could be better? We can evaluate parent as list and keep best regardless of optimizer n.
   best_a=None; best_score=inf
   def consider(a):
      nonlocal best_a,best_score
      if a is None: return
      a_norm=_norm(a)
      if a_norm is None: return
      if a_norm.size != n:
          # evaluate original; but optimizer only n. We can keep if length ok <=10000? But best_a length must match? final return any.
          sc=_score_eval(a_norm)
          if sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_a=a_norm.copy()
      else:
          sc=_score_eval(a_norm)
          if sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_a=a_norm.copy()
   # evaluate left
   consider(a_left)
   if par_arr is not None:
       consider(par_arr)
   # maybe evaluate left for a few other n? We can generate if time? But best_a may length different. We still use full optimizer for n.
   if best_score is inf or not finite:
       best_a=a_left; best_score=2.0

   # Ensure best_a length n for optimizer; if best length different (parent maybe), use left for optimizer
   a_opt = best_a.copy() if best_a is not None and best_a.size==n else a_left.copy()

   # seeds for L-BFGS: left, noisy left, right-boosted, parent
   seeds=[a_left.copy(), a_opt.copy()]
   # noisy
   for sigma in [0.05,0.1]:
       nz = a_left * np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,n)); seeds.append(_norm(nz))
   # right boost
   x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
   rb = a_left * np.exp(0.5*x**2); seeds.append(_norm(rb))
   rb2 = a_left * np.exp(0.5*(1-x)**2); seeds.append(_norm(rb2))
   # maybe left endpoint taper
   te = a_left * np.exp(-0.5*(1-x)**4); seeds.append(_norm(te))
   # unique/valid
   unique=[]; for s in seeds: if s is not None and s.size==n and not in unique: append

   # L-BFGS polish function
   def polish(a0, beta_fact, dur_s):
       phase_deadline=time.monotonic()+dur_s
       a0=_norm(a0)
       if a0 is None: return a0, inf
       M0=conv_same(a0).max()
       if not finite or M0<=0: return a0, _score_eval(a0)
       beta=float(min(1e7, max(1e3, beta_fact / M0)))  # beta_fact maybe 50 => beta=50/M0
       # objective closure
       zz_mean_lam=1e-10
       obj_count=0
       def fun(z):
           nonlocal obj_count
           obj_count += 1
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
               return 1e100, np.zeros_like(z)
           # clip z to avoid overflow?
           zc = np.clip(z, -50, 50)  # width 100 prevents under/overflow; but may constrain dynamics. Since optimal z range maybe [-10,10]. OK.
           amax = np.max(zc)
           a = np.exp(zc - amax)
           s = a.sum()
           if s <= 1e-300:
               return 1e100, np.zeros_like(z)
           a /= s
           C = conv_same(a)
           m = float(C.max())
           if not np.isfinite(m): return 1e100, zeros
           # If many C extremely close, c max 0
           # To avoid overflow if beta*(C-m) positive due rounding, subtract max
           c = beta * (C - m)
           c = np.maximum(c, -700.0) # but max should <=0; can be small positive from m? use c -= np.max(c)
           c_max = float(c.max())
           if c_max > 0: c -= c_max
           w = np.exp(c)
           zw = float(w.sum())
           if zw <= 0: w = np.zeros_like(C); w[int(np.argmax(C))]=1.0; zw=1; M=m
           else:
           M = m + math.log(zw)/beta
           w /= zw
           gM = cross(a,w)
           dot = float(np.dot(a,gM))
           h = gM - dot
           mz = float(np.mean(zc))
           gradz = a * (h)  # derivative of M wrt z
           if zz_mean_lam:
               gradz = gradz + 2.0*zz_mean_lam*mz/n
           val = M + zz_mean_lam*mz*mz
           if not np.isfinite(val): val=1e100
           # ensure grad finite
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(gradz)): gradz=np.nan_to_num(gradz)
           return val, gradz
       # initial z
       z0=np.log(a0+1e-300); z0 -= z0.mean(); z0=np.clip(z0,-30,30)
       # Use minimize
       try:
          res=spopt.minimize(fun, z0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
              options={'maxiter':5000,'maxfun':10000,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':50,
                       'callback':[lambda x: time.monotonic()>phase_deadline], 'disp':False})
       except Exception:
          return a0, _score_eval(a0)
       ares=None
       if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
           zc=np.clip(res.x,-50,50); amax=zc.max(); ares=np.exp(zc-amax); s=ares.sum(); if s>0: ares/=s
       if ares is None: ares=a0
       sc=_score_eval(ares)
       return ares, sc
```

The `M = m + log(zw)/beta`; if w at max 1, zw≥1. If β large, M approx m. Good.

But the gradient of Msoft with shifted c: derivative ∂(1/β log zw)/∂a = ∑ w dC, independent of shift. w normalized. Good.

One subtlety: Msoft as defined `m + logsumexp/c?` has derivative not exactly ∑w dC if m is the maximum which is a nonsmooth function. But the logsumexp of β(C-m) + m is exactly logsumexp(βC)/β? Let's verify: (1/β)log∑exp(βC) = m + (1/β)log∑exp(β(C-m)). Its derivative = (1/β)*(∑β exp(β(C-m)) dC / ∑exp) + m'. The m' term cancels due derivative of logsum? When we compute derivative as ∑w dC (without m'), that's exactly derivative of the first term; the full derivative of shifted expression is same as nonshifted because derivative of logsumexp independent of shift. The m' cancellation is inherent in the formula? Let's check if m is max, w normalized. For differentiable logsumexp with variables C_i and shift m(x): L = m + (1/β)log∑e^{β(C_i-m)}. ∂L = ∂m + (1/β)∑ e_i(β∂C_i - β∂m) /Z = ∂m + ∑w∂C_i - (∂m)∑w = ∑w∂C_i. Yes. Our w from e_i normalized, good. So gradient exact.

Subgradient refine:
```
def subgradient(a_in, max_time):
   a=_norm(a_in); best_local=a.copy(); best_local_M=approxM(a)
   lr=0.12
   it=0
   t_end=time.monotonic()+max_time
   while time<t_end:
      C=conv_same(a); M=C.max()
      if M < best_local_M: best_local=a.copy(); best_local_M=M
      # active threshold adaptive
      active = C >= M - 0.03*M
      cnt=active.sum()
      if cnt==0: active[np.argmax(C)]=True; cnt=1
      # maybe if cnt > n*2? no
      w=np.zeros(2*n-1); w[active]=1/cnt
      g=cross(a,w); h=g - np.dot(a,g)
      # random active subset occasionally
      if it % 1000 == 999:
          # pick min(cnt,512) random active indices
          idx=np.where(active)[0]
          if len(idx)>512: idx=rng.choice(idx,512,replace=False)
          w.fill(0); w[idx]=1/len(idx); g=cross(a,w); h=g-np.dot(a,g)
      step = lr*h
      a_new = a*np.exp(np.clip(-step,-6,6))
      a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-300); sm=a_new.sum(); if sm<=0: a_new=a; else a_new/=sm
      Cnew=conv_same(a_new); Mnew=Cnew.max()
      accept=False
      if Mnew < M - 1e-15: accept=True; lr=min(2*lr,1.0)
      elif Mnew < M * 1.005: accept=True
      else: lr*=0.6; if lr<1e-5: a=best_local.copy(); lr=0.12; 
      if accept: a=a_new; M=Mnew
      it +=1
      if it % 500 ==0:
          sc=_score_eval(a)
          if sc < best_local_score? Need exact local.
   return best_local, _score_eval(best_local)
```

```
def random_refine(a_best, time_left):
   t_end=...
   base = a_best.copy()
   for i in range(5000):
      if time>t_end break
      cand=base.copy()
      mode=rng.integers(3)
      if mode==0: # block multiplicative
         width=rng.integers(10, n//10)
         start=rng.integers(0,n-width)
         cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.2, width))
      elif mode==1: # global lognormal
         cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n))
      else: # tail boost
         x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
         cand *= np.exp(0.5*rng.normal(0,0.3)*x**2)
      cand=np.maximum(cand,1e-300); cand/=cand.sum()
      C=conv_same(cand); M=C.max()
      if 2*n*M < best_n_score - 1e-8:
          sc=_score_eval(cand); if sc<best_n_score: update
   return best_n
```

Let's implement `_safe_eval`:
```
def _safe_eval(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
        if arr.size == 0: return float('inf')
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.sum() <= 0: return float('inf')
        s = evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())
        return float(s)
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
```
List conversion of array length 4806. OK.

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    try:
      arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
    except: return None
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0: return None
    arr = arr / s
    arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0) # sum 1 so no
    # if any underflow? ok
    return arr
```
Since sum 1, cap irrelevant.

`_approx_score`:
```
def _approx_score(a, conv_same):
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return inf
    c=conv_same(a)
    return 2.0*a.size * float(c.max())/(s*s)
```
In loops normalized s=1, but safe.

Let's design seed generation:
```
x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
base_b=_left_flat_unnorm(n)
base_norm=base_b/base_b.sum()
seeds=[]
for eta in [-3,-1,0,1,2,4,8]:
   a=base_b*np.exp(eta*x)
   seeds.append(_norm(a))
# polynomial tail weights
for p,alpha in [(0.5,1),(1,1),(2,1),(0.5,2)]:
   a=base_b*(1+alpha*x**p)
   seeds.append(_norm(a))
# shifted singular
for cf in [0.05,0.1,0.2]:
   p=cf*(n-1)
   a=np.array([1.0/np.sqrt(1+abs(i-p)**(1.5)) for i in range(n)]) # maybe memory loop
   seeds.append(_norm(a))
# parent if norm
```

For LP max 1^T x s.t. A x ≤ b, x≥0. Feasible x. The dual min b^T y s.t. A^T y ≥1. The complementary slackness: if x_j>0 then (A^T y)_j=1; if constraint tight then y_i>0.
One iterative algorithm: start y_i small. For each variable j, compute u_j = A^T y_j. Increase y_i for constraints with load? This is like subgradient of dual. Maybe use:
- Given x, set dual y_i = λ (L_i/b_i) for L_i>M? Normalize.
- Update x_j *= u_j / average.
This is multiplicative weights approximation. Let's not risk.

Penalty projected gradient doable:
```
def lp_mw(f, M, max_iter, deadline):
   g=f.copy()
   fa = rfft(f,N)
   rho=10/M? Since load~M, violations small. Need scale.
   step=0.5
   for it:
      fg = irfft(fa*rfft(g,N))[:2n-1]
      V=np.maximum(fg - M,0)
      corr = cross(f, V)/2   # gradient of load
      grad = ones - 2*rho*corr
      # Adam on grad
      # projected: g = np.maximum(g + step*adam, 1e-300)
      # maybe rescale to keep sum not exploding
   return g
```

It does not contain a_k² because convolution coefficient k: term i=k, k-i=0 gives a_k a0, not a_k² (a_k² appears in C_{2k}). So each C_k is linear in every individual coefficient a_j (for j≤k), except? Yes C_k = ∑_{i+j=k} a_i a_j. For a fixed j, terms i=j and i=k-j; if k-j=j (k=2j), the term is a_j²? Wait i=j and k-j=j are the same index pair, but the sum over i includes i=j once, product a_j*a_j =a_j². In convolution definition, for i=j, term is a_j*a_j. So C_{2j} has a_j². Thus C_k is quadratic in a_j if 2j=k, otherwise linear. So not all linear.

But coordinate optimization still possible; for a_j, C_{2j} quadratic, others linear. Nonconvex due quadratic positive. Could do 1D minimax per coordinate.

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize as spopt


def _next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _make_fft_ops(n: int):
    N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    def conv_same(a):
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
        return np.ascontiguousarray(c[: 2 * n - 1])

    def cross(a, w):
        # Computes 2 * sum_{r=0}^{n-1} a[r] * w[j+r] for j=0..n-1.
        ar = np.ascontiguousarray(a[::-1])
        fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
        fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
        cw = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, N)
        return 2.0 * np.ascontiguousarray(cw[n - 1 : 2 * n - 1])

    return N, conv_same, cross


def _left_flat(n: int):
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * ((2 * k - 1) / (2.0 * k))
    return a


def _normalize(a):
    try:
        x = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if x.size == 0:
        return None
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    x = x / s
    return x


def _safe_eval(a):
    try:
        x = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(x.sum()) <= 0.0:
            return float("inf")
        return float(evaluate_sequence(x.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _approx_score(a, conv_same):
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    c = conv_same(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    return 2.0 * float(a.size) * m / (s * s)


def _polish_lbfgs(a0, conv_same, cross, n, beta_factor, duration_s, maxfun, hard_deadline):
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    if a0 is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    now = time.monotonic()
    end = min(now + max(1.0, float(duration_s)), hard_deadline - 5.0)
    if end <= now:
        return a0, _safe_eval(a0)

    c0 = conv_same(a0)
    m0 = float(c0.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m0) or m0 <= 0.0:
        return a0, _safe_eval(a0)

    beta = float(min(1.0e7, max(1.0e3, beta_factor / m0)))
    lambda_mean = 1.0e-10
    obj_count = 0

    def fun(z):
        nonlocal obj_count
        obj_count += 1
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
        if z.size != n:
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        amax = float(np.max(z))
        with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            a = np.exp(z - amax)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1.0e-300:
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a = a / s

        c = conv_same(a)
        m = float(c.max())
        if not math.isfinite(m):
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        cdiff = beta * (c - m)
        cdiff = np.nan_to_num(cdiff, nan=-700.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-700.0)
        cmax = float(cdiff.max())
        if cmax > 0.0:
            cdiff = cdiff - cmax

        with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            w = np.exp(cdiff)
        zw = float(w.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(zw) or zw <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            imx = int(np.argmax(c))
            w[imx] = 1.0
            zw = 1.0
        M = m + math.log(zw) / beta
        w = w / zw

        gM = cross(a, w)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gM)):
            gM = np.nan_to_num(gM, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        dot = float(np.dot(a, gM))
        h = gM - dot

        mz = float(np.mean(z))
        gradz = a * h + (2.0 * lambda_mean * mz) / float(n)
        gradz = np.nan_to_num(gradz, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        val = M + lambda_mean * mz * mz
        if not math.isfinite(val):
            val = 1.0e100
        return val, gradz

    z0 = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1.0e-300))
    z0 = z0 - float(z0.mean())
    z0 = np.nan_to_num(z0, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    stop = lambda x: time.monotonic() >= end
    try:
        res = spopt.minimize(
            fun,
            z0,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": max(20, int(maxfun) // 2),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "maxls": 25,
                "ftol": 1.0e-14,
                "gtol": 1.0e-8,
                "disp": False,
                "callback": stop,
            },
        )
    except Exception:
        return a0, _safe_eval(a0)

    out = a0
    if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
        zx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if zx.size == n:
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(zx)):
                zx = np.nan_to_num(zx, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            amax = float(np.max(zx))
            with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
                ax = np.exp(zx - amax)
            sx = float(ax.sum())
            if math.isfinite(sx) and sx > 1.0e-300:
                ax = ax / sx
                if np.all(np.isfinite(ax)) and ax.size == n:
                    out = ax

    sc = _safe_eval(out)
    return out, sc


def _subgradient_refine(a_in, conv_same, cross, n, end_time, rng, current_best_score):
    a = _normalize(a_in)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(current_best_score)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = _safe_eval(a)

    lr = 0.22
    it = 0
    stuck = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        c = conv_same(a)
        m = float(c.max())
        if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            break

        tol = max(0.025 * m, 1.0e-18)
        thresh = m - tol
        active = c >= thresh
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(active))
        if cnt == 0:
            active = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=bool)
            active[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
            cnt = 1
        if cnt > int(0.92 * (2 * n - 1)):
            active = c >= (m - max(0.007 * m, 1.0e-18))
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(active))
            if cnt == 0:
                active = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=bool)
                active[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
                cnt = 1

        w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        w[active] = 1.0 / float(cnt)

        g = cross(a, w)
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        dot = float(np.dot(a, g))
        h = g - dot
        hmax = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
        if hmax <= 1.0e-14:
            # Add tiny positive noise to unstick exact plateaus.
            p = a.size
            h += rng.normal(0.0, 1.0e-5, size=p)
            dot = float(np.dot(a, h))
            h = h - dot
            hmax = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
        h = h / max(1.0e-14, hmax)

        cand = a * np.exp(np.clip(-lr * h, -6.0, 6.0))
        cand = np.maximum(cand, 1.0e-300)
        scand = float(cand.sum())
        if scand <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(scand):
            cand = a.copy()
            } else:
            cand /= scand

        cn = conv_same(cand)
        mn = float(cn.max())

        accepted = False
        if mn < m - 1.0e-15:
            accepted = True
            lr = min(2.0 * lr, 1.2)
            stuck = 0
        elif mn < m * 1.003:
            accepted = True
            stuck += 1
        else:
            lr *= 0.6
            stuck += 1

        if accepted:
            a = cand
            m = mn

        if stuck > 400:
            a = best_a.copy()
            lr = 0.22
            stuck = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 1200 == 0 or m < best_score / (2.0 * n) - 1.0e-9:
            sc = _safe_eval(a)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()

    sc = _safe_eval(best_a)
    if math.isfinite(sc):
        return best_a, sc
    return best_a, float("inf")


def _random_refine(a_in, conv_same, cross, n, end_time, rng, current_best_score):
    base = _normalize(a_in)
    if base is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    best_a = base.copy()
    best_score = float(current_best_score)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = _safe_eval(base)

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = idx / max(1.0, float(n - 1))

    t_end = max(time.monotonic() + 1.0, end_time)
    k = 0
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        cand = base.copy()
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 5))

        if mode == 0:
            width = int(rng.integers(max(8, n // 400), max(16, n // 18)))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            stop = min(n, start + width)
            cand[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.18, size=stop - start))
        elif mode == 1:
            cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.06, size=n))
        elif mode == 2:
            cand *= np.exp(0.7 * rng.normal(0.0, 0.35) * (x ** 2))
        elif mode == 3:
            cand *= np.exp(0.7 * rng.normal(0.0, 0.35) * ((1.0 - x) ** 2))
        else:
            b = _left_flat(n)
            cand = 0.6 * base + 0.4 * b / b.sum()
            cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, size=n))

        cand = np.maximum(cand, 1.0e-300)
        s = float(cand.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
            continue
        cand /= s

        sc_est = _approx_score(cand, conv_same)
        if math.isfinite(sc_est) and sc_est < best_score - 1.0e-7:
            sc = _safe_eval(cand)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = cand.copy()
                base = cand.copy()

        k += 1
        if k % 900 == 0:
            base = best_a.copy()

    sc = _safe_eval(best_a)
    if math.isfinite(sc):
        return best_a, sc
    return best_a, float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        B = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        B = 1300.0
    if not math.isfinite(B) or B <= 1:
        B = 1300.0
    deadline = t_start + max(10.0, B) - 10.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent_arr = None
    try:
        p = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        p = None
    if p is not None:
        try:
            par = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if par.size > 0:
                par = np.where(np.isfinite(par), par, 0.0)
                par = np.clip(par, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if float(par.sum()) >= 0.01:
                    parent_arr = par
        except Exception:
            parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is not None and 64 <= parent_arr.size <= 8000:
        n = int(parent_arr.size)
    else:
        n = 4806
    n = int(max(64, min(8000, n)))

    N, conv_same, cross = _make_fft_ops(n)

    base = _left_flat(n)
    base = _normalize(base)
    if base is None:
        base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n

    best_n_a = base.copy()
    best_n_score = _safe_eval(best_n_a)
    best_g_a = base.copy()
    best_g_score = best_n_score

    if parent_arr is not None:
        sc_p = _safe_eval(parent_arr)
        if math.isfinite(sc_p) and sc_p < best_g_score:
            best_g_score = sc_p
            best_g_a = parent_arr.copy()

    if best_g_score < best_n_score and len(best_g_a) == n and math.isfinite(best_g_score):
        best_n_a = _normalize(best_g_a)
        best_n_score = best_g_score

    # Build several structured seeds.
    seeds = [base.copy()]
    braw = _left_flat(n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = idx / max(1.0, float(n - 1))

    for eta in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        seeds.append(braw * np.exp(eta * x))

    for powv, alp in ((0.5, 1.0), (1.0, 1.0), (2.0, 1.0), (1.0, 3.0)):
        seeds.append(braw * (1.0 + alp * (x ** powv)))

    for powv, alp in ((1.0, 1.0), (2.0, 2.0)):
        seeds.append(braw / (1.0 + alp * ((1.0 - x) ** powv)))

    for cent_frac in (0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.35):
        cpos = cent_frac * max(1.0, float(n - 1.0))
        seeds.append(1.0 / np.sqrt(1.0 + np.abs(idx - cpos) ** 1.5))

    # Remove duplicates/invalids and keep the best few by a cheap FFT score.
    unique = []
    seen = 0
    for a in seeds:
        an = _normalize(a)
        if an is None or an.size != n:
            continue
        if seen == 0:
            unique.append(an.copy())
            seen += 1
            continue
        ok = True
        for u in unique:
            if float(np.linalg.norm(an - u)) < 1.0e-5:
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            unique.append(an.copy())

    scored = []
    for a in unique:
        sc_est = _approx_score(a, conv_same)
        if math.isfinite(sc_est):
            scored.append((sc_est, a))
    scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    top_seeds = [a for _, a in scored[: min(4, len(scored))]]
    if not top_seeds:
        top_seeds = [base.copy()]

    for a in top_seeds:
        sc = _safe_eval(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_n_score:
            best_n_score = sc
            best_n_a = a.copy()
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_g_score:
            best_g_score = sc
            best_g_a = a.copy()

    # Alternating smooth L-BFGS refinements.
    phases = (
        (10.0, 45.0, 900),
        (40.0, 55.0, 1100),
        (160.0, 70.0, 1300),
        (600.0, 70.0, 1300),
    )
    seed_cycle = 0
    for beta_fact, dur, maxfun in phases:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 260.0:
            break
        if seed_cycle == 0:
            a0 = best_n_a
        else:
            a0 = top_seeds[min(seed_cycle - 1, len(top_seeds) - 1)]

        a_new, sc_new = _polish_lbfgs(a0, conv_same, cross, n, beta_fact, dur, maxfun, deadline)
        a_new = _normalize(a_new)
        if a_new is not None and math.isfinite(sc_new):
            if sc_new < best_n_score - 1.0e-12:
                best_n_score = sc_new
                best_n_a = a_new.copy()
                seed_cycle = 0
            else:
                seed_cycle += 1
            if sc_new < best_g_score:
                best_g_score = sc_new
                best_g_a = a_new.copy()

    # Tailored projected-subgradient polishing.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
        sub_end = min(deadline - 35.0, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.55))
        a_sub, sc_sub = _subgradient_refine(best_n_a, conv_same, cross, n, sub_end, rng, best_n_score)
        a_sub = _normalize(a_sub)
        if a_sub is not None and math.isfinite(sc_sub):
            if sc_sub < best_n_score - 1.0e-12:
                best_n_score = sc_sub
                best_n_a = a_sub.copy()
            if sc_sub < best_g_score:
                best_g_score = sc_sub
                best_g_a = a_sub.copy()

    # Late random/local sweet-spot search.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        a_rand, sc_rand = _random_refine(
            best_n_a,
            conv_same,
            cross,
            n,
            deadline - 12.0,
            rng,
            best_g_score,
        )
        a_rand = _normalize(a_rand)
        if a_rand is not None and math.isfinite(sc_rand):
            if sc_rand < best_n_score - 1.0e-12:
                best_n_score = sc_rand
                best_n_a = a_rand.copy()
            if sc_rand < best_g_score:
                best_g_score = sc_rand
                best_g_a = a_rand.copy()

    final_a = best_g_a
    final_score = best_g_score

    # Final exact verification; keep a valid fallback.
    if final_a is None or len(final_a) == 0 or not math.isfinite(final_score):
        final_a = best_n_a
        final_score = _safe_eval(final_a)
    else:
        sc_final = _safe_eval(final_a)
        if math.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < final_score:
            final_score = sc_final
        elif not math.isfinite(final_score):
            final_score = _safe_eval(final_a)

    fallback_score = _safe_eval(best_n_a)
    if (not math.isfinite(final_score)) and math.isfinite(fallback_score):
        final_a = best_n_a
        final_score = fallback_score

    if final_a is None or len(final_a) == 0:
        final_a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
        final_score = _safe_eval(final_a)

    fa = np.asarray(final_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    fa = np.where(np.isfinite(fa), fa, 0.0)
    fa = np.clip(fa, 0.0, 1000.0)
    sfa = float(fa.sum())
    if sfa <= 0.01 or not math.isfinite(sfa):
        fa = _left_flat(n) if n is not None else np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
        fa = np.where(np.isfinite(fa), fa, 0.0)
        fa = np.clip(fa, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(fa.sum()) <= 0.01:
            fa = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(v) for v in fa.tolist()]
```
